## The Caul Hill fight (D+48), as staged

**Terrain:** room 1 (above). The door is in the north wall; the corridor outside runs
east–west. The lifter lies over the carrier and sledge by the east-wall boxes, its hump
toward the corner heap. The floor rope enters over the threshold; the ceiling rope
through the hole above the door and down the inside wall; three arm-ropes lie out across
the boxes and floor. Heaps: the south-east corner; by the far cabinet; and in the
corridor by the steel door. The counter is on Dee's shoulder (off Callie's hip).

**Positions:** Callie just inside the threshold facing the corridor, hook still on the
threshold, lee up into the corridor and leaned back up the wall over the hole — **a door
and a bit**, with her back to the room. Dee by the cabinets near the door (glass, open
jar, vent-jar bag on a cabinet top). Jab crouched and moving, frame on a cabinet top.
Toren between the lifter and the others. A lantern on the corridor floor lights Callie's
side; one hangs from the frame.

**Sequence (Dee's count; a three and ten ≈ one glass turn). Repaired staging: Toren holds
Guard or Edge, never both.**
1. On a ten: Toren cuts the **ceiling rope outside the hole**, then the **floor rope at
   the threshold** (heel drag, "like a sack of flour opened with a hook"), steps in and
   **lets the heel go dark**. Dee and Jab in; Callie last; lee set. **Isolation.**
2. First three: the corridor stump and the ceiling stump push, heap against the lee's
   outside, and cannot cross. An arm-rope swings at Callie's back; **Toren's Guard strut
   takes it** ("a sack hitting a wall"). Two husks stand in the room. **Catch 1:** Jab's
   palm on the room-side floor stump — one push, a palm's worth, "and then nothing
   behind it". The stump does not grow.
3. Toren pins the cabinet husk on a strut; Jab kills the corner husk (hook of the
   fish-knife lit) and **catches the corner heap** at the next three — no husk stands.
   **Jar 2 runs out during this catch; jar 3 opened, "the last one above the line."**
   Principle stated: *it doesn't matter where; it's all out of the drum* → Dee: *pick the
   one.*
4. **The lifter adapts:** it pushes into the pinned husk to make it heavier; Jab catches
   at the husk's foot. Then it **throws** the second arm-rope at Jab; Toren holds **two
   struts** (husk and a roof over Jab and Dee). Then it pushes to mend the struck rope
   instead of the stump Jab is on (a wrong pick).
5. **Toren lets the husk strut go (the husk falls on its front), lights, cuts the near
   arm-rope, darkens, and re-sets the strut as the husk rises**; "The three's in four
   breaths"; Jab catches at the stump (4 caught; first vent jar full); it stays cut.
6. **Catch 7 late:** the ten draws half the palm's worth back out of Jab and him "after
   it"; after that he calls *off* before Dee.
7. Callie **looks round once** (Rook's prediction); Jab says "Busy"; she turns back. A
   corridor husk is pressed against the outside of her lee; blood on her lip.
8. The hump shrinks to knee height (eleventh catch); the husk to "the size of a boy";
   the three stays open longer (five breaths) — pumping itself dry.
9. **At the seventeenth three the lifter drags itself along the floor stump toward the
   door** — toward the river on the other side of Callie. Toren **lets the husk strut go**
   and sets one strut in front of the lifter, a pace from Callie's heels; it **leans**
   ("That's the cart"). "That's the last of the stone. I'm on mine… Two turns of mine…
   One strut." The husk rises and comes at Jab; **Jab kills it at the end of a ten and
   catches its grey at the three**; it does not stand again.
10. "Jar" at the eighteenth or nineteenth three: **the line.** Dee asks Jab "What do you
    feel?" ("It's missing… nearly dry… one more, maybe two"); reads 130, not red; "Say
    it." Toren's crossing ("inside two turns… This is once"); Dee's co-signature and
    price; **jar 4 opened under the line.** Callie: "My stone went at the fifteenth
    three. I'm on my own. I've got two turns. I've not got three."
11. The open stays open. Jab puts his palm **on the lifter itself** and takes the push
    and holds on through the open, the clean cold pulling hard. Toren will not light with
    the strut up: **"Callie. I'm giving you the lean." — "Give it me." — "Letting go."**
    The lifter slides the last pace against the room side of her still curve and stops;
    "Mine." Toren lights a finger of the **point** off his own and **puts it into the open
    vent** before the ten. It goes out "like a pinched wick" and falls apart into a
    knee-deep slope of loose grey. He lets the point go dark ("At the end. Not after").
    Jab comes off and jars his last palm's worth.

**Kills:** D+47 — the corner husk three times in room 1 (the third after Toren releases
its strut), one husk at the steel door, one against Callie's Ward at the stair top.
D+48 — the corner husk once (Jab), the released cabinet husk (Jab, caught at the three),
then the lifter; every husk it had made came apart when it died. **No spawn reconstituted after
the kill.**

## Recovered boxes

- **Two live wheel boxes** in the lead carrier, lid in its groove, on the tailboard
  sledge, on the cart bed behind the home Flasks (loaded by tipping the cart).
  - Box 1: 110 at a hand, slide shut (room 1, D+47).
  - Box 2: 106 at a hand, slide shut (room 1, D+48).
- **Not taken:** the 140 box (slide rusted half-open). Other live boxes may lie in the
  unread part of room 1's heap and in rooms 2–11; nobody read further ("a quarter of an
  hour on her knees… she could not do it").
- The carrier's tongs are grey to the rings and were left in room 1 (not stated as
  brought out).

## Bodies at exit (D+49 morning)

- **Pell:** right ankle broken, splinted with the tailboard rail and canvas strips; the
  swelling pressed the strips and Dee loosened one; toes pink. Sits up on the cart. Cannot
  walk. Counted the sky for two days.
- **Rook:** the reopened back is **infected** ("the dog"): hot, red a hand's width round
  the stitches, shiny, not spreading since D+47 night. Fever highest D+47; cooler D+48;
  "holding" D+49. **Sowerby's fever powder: three papers used, one left.** Off. On D+48 he
  dragged two wrack poles onto the bridge on his knees ("That's not working. That's
  furniture"). Rides on the cart on his side.
- **Toren:** left forearm in its cuff, jarred twice in M4, **not used as a lever in M5**.
  Own two turns D+48 morning; to his floor D+48; "a turn" D+49 morning, "two by tonight if you do
  nothing". Stone empty.
- **Callie:** **left ribs cracked — two, the same place** — falling at her floor onto her
  own still-fixed conduit after the lee went out (D+48); she did not belay afterwards
  (the ring and rail post held the back rope); strapped D+49; no lifting heavier than a counter for a
  week; hook as a walking stick. Nosebleed D+48. Own reserve half D+49. Stone empty.
- **Jab:** **right palm: a coin-sized white, shiny, fixed patch on the heel** from the
  D+47 sink; red edge and a small blister by D+49; he can draw with the rest of the hand
  "round it". Arm warm to the elbow D+47, drawn. Palms warm after the D+48 solo draws,
  drawn cool by Dee D+49. Floor a finger; own about a hand. Did not go below his floor.
- **Dee:** **at her floor once** (D+48) after being the watch for every catch with nobody
  drawing her; **sick in the night** (vomiting, shaking — twenty turns in the room at
  110–130 undrawn); drawn by Jab. D+49: a finger over her floor; can watch, not draw
  "today". Says of it: "I'll not do it that way again." Rook: "Yes you will."
- **Duchess:** sound; eating; not taken across the river.

## Object state at exit

- **Cart** (whole, heading to the cut): three home Flasks; the carrier on the tailboard
  sledge with two live boxes; Jab's frame in the rack behind the driver's bench (Jab's
  blistered palm); canvas; Rook; Pell; the empty road jar; two skins (drinking water
  partly used; D+48 and D+49 pots boiled from wrack). The tailboard is the sledge; the
  tailboard rail is Pell's splint. Traces are rope.
- **Jab's frame:** fence jar 4 (5), fence jar 5 (8), Jab's lifter jars (8, 4½), Callie's
  (8; and 4 dirty in B's old socket, broken glass removed), Toren's lifter jar (empty).
- **Left at Caul Hill:** in room 1, three full stoppered vent jars under the racks with a
  chalk cross; the tongs; the 140 box and the rest of the heap; the lifter's ash across the
  floor, the corridor's collapsed rope-bank and the full tray. At the pump house: the
  buried grip rag under a chalk-crossed stone, with the two D+47 vent jars beside it.
  Pell's two wrack poles on the bridge over the cracked plank.
- **The gate:** chained, joined by the green copper twist, ends left loose.
- **The steel door:** standing open against the lobby wall.
- **Callie's hook:** curve washed (11); grip bare steel; *pen, 35th* tag kept "so they
  can write under it".
- **Toren's signed paper, back:** D+47 and D+48 counts (*Out: 4. In: 4*), Callie's and
  Pell's sky counts, *The lifter*, the ash, *J.'s right palm… fixed*, *It mends in the
  three*, *Gran's door pulls. From the other side it pushes*, the D+48 record (under the
  line on purpose, inside 2 turns, 3 Handfuls, jar 4, T. and D. W.'s call, *Once. For the
  Director.*; C. holding the lean at the last; two boxes; the third left, open; injuries; *The
  sky's lost the count*), *Its own: a Flask (R.). What it had: more than we could lift*,
  and *49th… Out: 6… The wash tomorrow, in pieces (Pell's). Home the 55th, if the wind's
  down.* Dessa's six blank lines for *back* are **not yet written in**. The copied plate
  is in his coat; the stone is not mentioned in this movement.
- **Dee:** glass; book; small bag (needle, thread; **one fever paper left**); Pell's
  chalk.
- **Little:** buttoned in Jab's coat, left side (not taken out on the page in M5; it
  appears as a touch through the cloth in the sink and the fight).

## Knowledge boundaries at exit

- **Known and shown:** the lifter's draw/push rhythm; that it pushes into any hole,
  including a drawing hand; that stoppered jars are beyond it; that a lee in a door stops
  crawling ash but not rope, and a cut rope plus a lee in the cut isolates a room; that
  catching "on the push" drains it; that its husks were its hands; that when it died the
  ropes stopped; that the sky **lost its count** the same afternoon and falls now come
  at no three.
- **Not known:** whether the sky kept the lifter's time or the lifter kept the sky's
  ("I don't know which" — Jab; "I'd like to know which" — Pell); what the falls are or
  what is in the drum; whether there are others like it; whether the loose ash on the
  yard and in the wash has **stopped moving** (not checked on the page outside room 1 and
  the corridor — Movement Six can show the drifts lying still, or not); how many live
  boxes remain in rooms 1–11.
- **Withheld:** Dee's door count; Rook's third door; Katori's words; the disk (Toren's
  stone is not mentioned in M5; **the single disk clue is held for Movement Six**); the
  Makers; Cinder.
- **The Director's permission:** kept as recorded above. The party never passed room 1.
  The count she asked for is on the paper (Callie's and Pell's).

## Route-home decision

Pell's options from D+46 still stand (north round the drifts' head toward the hill; or
south through the marsh and down this bank — two days more either way, no bed for the
cart; home about **D+57 or later**). On D+49 Pell turns his own "Nothing takes a cart
across that" into **"A cart in pieces goes across in pieces"**: wheels rolled down and up
the gap, the axle carried by two, the bed by four or two and the horse on a rope, the
shafts one each, the carrier's board slid over the drift ("it's flour"), Toren's strut
steps on the loose banks, Pell on a canvas litter, Duchess led through. Callie prices it:
**an hour at ~80, all of them and the horse, about 10–12 of the lifter's**. Toren: **the
wash, in pieces, tomorrow at first light if the wind is down; not today** ("Nobody
carries a wheel through a drift at their floor"); if the wind is up, wait at the cut and
write the cost. Dee writes it. They move the whole cart a mile to the cut on D+49. Home
**D+55** if the crossing goes on D+50 and the road holds (five days from east of the gap).
**The crossing itself is not drafted.**

## Open clocks and exact exit pressure for Movement Six

- **Where and when:** the cut on the west side of the collapsed wash crown, D+49
  afternoon/evening; the crossing planned for first light D+50, wind permitting.
- **Machine:** the next cycle is about **D+54**; the party cannot be home before
  **D+55**. "Nothing's the fifty-fourth. The fifty-fourth was gone before we crossed the
  bridge." Sowerby can dose Senna off her blood "for a few cycles. Badly." The part is
  on the cart; the repair is not done.
- **Ash:** lifter's 20½ clean before the crossing (~10½ after), fence 13, dirty 4, home 24
  untouched. Nobody carries settled ash at exit. Toren must settle for his strut-steps.
- **The breach:** the deliberate crossing of the signed line (3 Handfuls, inside two
  turns, Toren's and Dee's call, "once") is on the paper *For the Director* and awaits
  her review. The standing rule "Two Flasks left inside: everybody out" is unchanged.
- **Bodies:** Rook's infection with one fever paper left; Pell carried; Callie's cracked
  ribs; Jab's fixed palm mark and a blister; Dee a finger over her floor; Toren's own
  recovering, forearm for Sowerby.
- **The rip:** the sky over Caul Hill has **lost its count** and still lets go falls. Pell:
  the Director will want that "more than the boxes, I think. Or as much." Movement Six's
  Sag surge from the distant rip remains available and is not foreshadowed further.
- **Meridian (not visited):** reviews held; Wyck cleared D+56; the technician's hand
  known ~D+57; Tonk and Tilda as at D+41; Dessa's six blank lines.

## Deliberately absent or withheld

- **No new continuing named character.** "The lifter" is a creature term. The shed's
  label-writer is not named.
- **No home Flask spent. No infinite ash, no soul-theft, no time reversal, no universal
  healing, no second hidden power.** The lifter never reached a stoppered jar or a settled
  stone; Ward stopped matter, not radiation (the lee did nothing about the room's
  reading); Sustain drew active burden only — Jab's palm mark, Callie's ribs, Pell's
  ankle and Rook's infection all remain; Guard was paid by the breath each time.
- **No pre-death windfall:** the lifter's ash was lifted only after it died; the crossing
  under the line was paid from fence jar 4.
- **No romance. No death.** Rook and Pell live and stay injured.
- **The east road is still cut; the return, the machine repair, the accord, patient
  outcomes and the north-yard school are unresolved.**

## Length and viewpoint (facts for review, not scores)

- **Length:** 47,586 words after repair (`wc -w`; first draft 46,193), inside the
  packet's approximate 42,000–48,000.
- **Viewpoint share, Movement Five (repaired):** Callie 36.5% (3 chapters, 17,391),
  Toren 35.3% (3, 16,795), Jab 28.2% (2, 13,400). **Owner ruling: allocation accepted;
  not rebalanced; Movement Six may lean toward Jab if the story permits.**
- **Cumulative through Movement Five** (270,387 words): Callie 34.5%, Toren 33.4%, Jab
  32.1%.
- **Scene breaks:** 105 `---` markers across 8 chapters after repair (106 in the first
  draft), about 453 words per section —
  short against the formula's ~950-word proxy, the same direction as Movements Two to
  Four.
- **Surface phrases watched:** "the way …" occurs about 141 times across the repaired
  movement (139 in the first draft)
  (including literal uses such as "the way home"), the same tic flagged and thinned in
  M4. "Nobody said anything" occurs 7 times. None of the M4 watched tics ("the woman
  with the good boots", "face did nothing at all, which was how", "tipped plate", "about
  four seconds", "grey-haired man") appears.
- **Formula comparison** (sentence distribution, Flesch, development-beat cadence,
  progression-vocabulary placement) was **not run**; it belongs to the editorial pass.

## PROJECT EVIDENCE — provenance/MOVEMENT-006-CONTINUITY.md

# Movement Six — continuity checkpoint

Author run: Claude Opus 5.5 (`claude-opus-5-5`), Monroe Jackson 1.3.0 / O'Connor 1.3.0.
This is a working note for movement review and Book Three planning. It is not
manuscript text and it is not an editorial score. It records the state the page leaves
behind, including what the page does *not* settle. Every number below was checked
against `manuscript/chapter-41.md` … `chapter-48.md` at the end of the drafting run;
the frozen copy is `editions/movement-006-first-draft/`.

## Conflicts resolved before or during drafting

| Question | Resolution on the page | Authority |
|---|---|---|
| Where the disk stands in Book Two | The disk (Book One: near-black, two thumbs wide, nine grooves each face, cold, on braided boot-lace, under the shirt) had **not been mentioned anywhere in chs 1–40**, and the Hobb's Wall record-office scene (*released to bearer*) was withheld in M1. The clue is therefore staged so it reads without that scene: an empty nine-ribbed socket in the machine's rack and a pencilled *released to bearer* in the machine's fittings book (ch 46), then Toren's private recognition (ch 48). **This is the first appearance of the phrase *released to bearer* in the Book Two manuscript.** | Packet §10; M1 checkpoint; Book One ch 9/14 |
| Which box is "the liar" | M1/M2 place the drifting reference in the **third row, fourth along**, with the fifth along "waking slow". The new box goes into that slot (ch 46) | M1, M2 checkpoints |
| How long the new box takes | Ch 9 says "a month's work. Less, if I didn't sleep." The technician has done that month by hand while waiting (numbers chalked beside every box, pages of the manual copied); with a live box the rack learns it in "a night, maybe two". Installed D+56, green at first light D+57 | Ch 9; author |
| Cinder's form on the page | Book One: joining fees paid in **stamped brass chits** (section name + number, e.g. TULL / 6). The trace is one such chit, new letters, not four, number not six, shown to Rook; **letters not given on the page** | Book One chs 30, 42, 46; packet |
| Who commands the return hold | Dessa, throughout. Toren states resources, asks what she wants, and assigns only within her orders (chs 44–45) | Packet: Dessa not displaced |
| Guard/Edge | One configuration at a time throughout. At the dark lane Toren releases → lights → kills → darkens → re-sets once alone; thereafter he **holds and Katori kills** (ch 45) | Owner ruling (M5) |
| Ward | Moves matter and bodies only: a still lean-to along the road edge against husks and heaped fog-ash; the still hook across the made road at the lip. It does nothing about burden; Jab draws that | Owner ruling (M4) |

## The wash crossing (D+50), as staged

- **Floors at dawn:** Toren stone full (1, settled D+49 night), own two turns and the bit;
  Callie own three parts, nothing settled; Jab a hand and a bit, floor a finger, the palm's
  blister dressed; **Dee two fingers over her floor — "I can watch. I can draw leftovers
  off one person, once. I can't work."** Rook hot again; off.
- **Assignments (Toren's, at the cart):** Toren first and in the bottom most; Callie reads
  banks, then crosses **once** leading Duchess and stays east; Jab crosses with all the ash
  and is the draw station on the east lip (40); Dee lowers loads round the west post and
  calls the glass, crosses last and draws Jab once; Rook off, crosses last, counts; Pell on
  the shafts with the home three.
- **Costs and events:** Duchess balks when the drift slides at no count; the rope comes
  tight; **Callie opens her hand** ("Let it run. Lead's cheaper than you"). The lost wheel
  (above). Toren drawn four times; everyone drawn once on the east lip; Duchess drawn.
  Nobody went under a floor.

## The return hold (D+55), as staged

**Terrain (from the M2–M3 canon):** the Sag a bowl ~300 paces lip to lip; the made road
straight down the north slope, across tilted slabs with black water in the joints, up the
south slope to the south lip; the east track with its lamp-lines; the dark lane between
lamps 3 and 4 running down toward the ditch; the west rim above the pipe's west mouth;
post 6 flat in the water; the dropped slab at the road's east edge beside it. Arrivals
come up on the east side, over the joints.

**Home line at their posts:** Dessa on the crate at the south lip, eyes shut, command; Katori
at the top of the dark lane (lit since noon); Ines on the west rim (bow); Otto and the two
yard lifters on the lamp-line with bars (one lifter down sick on a canvas behind the lamps);
the weighbridge girl on the lip path (blade right-handed; plaster off, leather binding);
Wyck on the lip above the lamps (bar left-handed, one day short of clearance); a gate-crew
man nursing the sick lifter. Dessa: 40 husks and hounds twice since noon; nothing through.

**Entry floors (Dee, at the north lip):** Toren stone 1 + own two turns and the bit; Callie
½ settled + own all back; Jab a hand and a bit; Dee a hand over her floor ("I'm a watch
again"). Jars: lifter's first ¼, Callie's first 7½; fence 13 (5 + 8); dirty 4; home 24.
Toren: "They go back on the board… Anything that gets drawn today comes out of the lifter's.
Then the fence's. Not those."

**Sequence:**
1. Toren states the party's state across the bowl and asks "*What do you want?*" Dessa:
   everyone over "without anything following you up", and her watch drawn. Her orders:
   the made road; **Callie on the ditch side from the corner, not before**; Toren where the
   wheel needs him; Jab calling; nobody stops in the bottom; on a push, stand still.
2. Down the quiet top half; a push mid-slope — everyone stands, nothing worked; the husk goes
   for the lamps; Ines kills it.
3. **The corner:** Callie kneels on the stone slab past the dropped one, still hook on its
   edge, bend east, one hand on the tail; a **lean-to along the road** the length of the cart
   and horse ("It's a line. It's not a door… It's dear"). Toren sets under the walking wheel
   from the stone slab ("Holding"). **A push arrives mid-drop**: three husks stand out of the
   joints and go for the worked things; two stand against the lee's outside; the third goes
   round the end — **Jab kills it with the lit hook**; Ines kills the two against the lee.
   Toren lets the strut go at the end. "Down." Callie: all the half and some of her own.
4. Up the south slope; the line opens; Otto and a lifter take Duchess. "Behind the lamps."
5. **The rotation** at the trestle table: Jab works, Dee watches and draws his leftovers each
   time; order by Dessa (sick lifter, Otto, Katori, the other lifter; then the girl, Wyck,
   Ines; Dessa is not busy — "It came up to thirty feet"). Replacements by handoff under
   Dessa's command: Wyck takes Otto's bar; **Toren takes the dark lane from Katori**
   ("*Taking*" / "Yours" / "Mine" / "*Letting go*").
6. **The lane:** Katori's instruction — *don't block it, narrow it*: a strut at a slant from
   the third-lamp side leaves a pace. A hound hits the strut and comes through the pace;
   Toren, alone, **releases, lights the heel, kills, darkens, re-sets**. Katori, drawn,
   returns by choice: "**You hold. I kill.**"
7. **The big push at dusk** ("It's all of it… more than two breaths"): the fog heaves up the
   whole bowl. The line holds behind the lamps; Wyck takes a husk on his bar and turns it
   off ("*Heels*"). Callie holds the still hook across the top of the made road above the
   draw station. Three hounds up the lane: Toren holds, Katori kills all three over the
   strut. **Toren's stone goes; he holds on his own till Katori says.**
8. At the table: *jar* → **the fence's fourth, not the home three**. Jab: "*Off. I'm warm.
   The palm. Round the mark. It's to the wrist… It's mine*"; vents long into the lip's
   dirty ground; sits; Dee draws him cool (the blister has broken). **He chooses to go back
   on** ("If you'll watch"); Dee: "Three. Then you're off." He draws the girl, Wyck, Ines.
9. Callie at her floor, not under ("*I'm stopping*") at the push's end; nosebleed. The fog lies
   down with no count; some husks come apart on their own, some go back down — nobody can
   tell which. Counter at the lip 40, slope 70 (110 in the push). Dessa counts everyone.

**Costs:** the second yard lifter bitten on the calf (bounded new wound); Callie's floor and
ribs; Toren's stone gone and his own to a turn; Jab's broken blister and a lodging stopped
at the wrist; Katori's burden since noon (drawn). No death. Nothing through.

  rail; 40 going in at the fence; 13 coming out; 2 more at the Sag → **11**. Home 24 untouched.
- **Back to the store: 35 clean** (home 24 + fence 11). **Spent of the store's: 61** —
  "the machine's", written by the Director as station spend, **not against any household**.
- The hill's: **28 clean lifted, all spent**; **4 dirty** to the dirty shelf marked *THE HILL*;
  "**the rest where it lies**" (knee-deep in room 1, the corridor bank, the full tray).
- **The Director's apportionment (read at the plate, D+58):** to the store 32 (home 24 +
  fence jar 5's 8); the fence's fourth (3) to the east shed, "for the treatment and the
  table" (medical); from the store by her order **2 Flasks to the Sag** "for the table behind
  the lamps, this winter" against *D. C.* (road/defence); **½ Flask clean + the 4 dirty to the
  north yard** for the lanes against *Wyck* (training). "Every jar against a name."
- The weighbridge board: the LENT line struck with a ruler; beneath it *BACK: HOME 3, NOT
  TOUCHED (T.'S KNOTS). FENCE 11 (1 WHOLE, 3). = 35 CLEAN*, the 61, the hill's 28/4/"the rest
  where it lies", and the apportionment. *SUSSTAIN* still boxed above.
- **Left in the field:** 3 full vent jars under room 1's racks (chalk cross); 2 D+47 vent jars
  by the pump-house stone; vent jar #1 in the siding ditch under a re-chalked stone.

## Machine repair and patient states

- **The machine:** the 110 box replaces the third-row fourth-along liar (removed with the
  shed's tongs to the bench); eleven back-plate screws turned by an assistant on the
  technician's instruction; yellow (learning) D+56 → **green D+57, every row agreeing** ("I've
  not seen that since I was twenty-six"). Warmed **once**; the note holds — Senna: "It's in
  tune." The **106 box** stays in its lead in the shed's locked cupboard by the Director's
  order: the spare and "the proof. You don't spend the proof." The fifth-along box's
  state is not re-read on the page.
- **The technician:** right hand in a sling, fingers curled, fixed ("It's left-handed from
  here"; Sowerby's verdict due D+57 is pre-empted). He wept at the green light (the Director:
  "I'd like that not repeated").
- **Senna:** dosed off her blood by Sowerby on D+54 ("a needle and a lot of language"): bruised
  both elbows, a fever D+55 she hid from Toren. The honest machine D+57: "a step back. Not
  more." Course back to about D+43's standing; **next cycle on time, D+65**, and every one after.
  Hands and feet unchanged, "It's not that kind of machine." Still lifted by two; "Mind the
  camber." Asks Toren about the door ("Thirty breaths… We did a quarter of an hour. Eleven of
  us"). In her chair in the north-yard corner D+59, giving reasons for Dessa's new column.
- **Tilda:** no fit since D+40 (17 days on D+57). Words slower, each with "a little space in
  front of it", but they come; she found *Home line* and tried it aloud. Right hand pins the
  tally board; on D+57 **closes most of the way on a cup, lifts it an inch shaking, drinks;
  cannot open it again except with her left hand.** Right leg lags. Picture D+57, by her
  consent ("Look. Don't tell me the number"): **swelling down (the second medicine); the growth
  where it was and the size it was.** "I wanted it said by somebody who wasn't me." Hand flat
  on the shut folder; Callie does not ask. Tallying at the weighbridge, now paid in the
  weighman's book.
- **Tonk:** bone clean; the frame till spring; the growth plate as it was. **Lowest pin site
  weeping since D+50** (skin), salt water twice daily; bites the cord. **Full chair transfer in
  6 minutes, no hand on him (D+56).** Keeps both ends of the cord; Little back on his pillow.
  Tells Jab to put supper first on the paper.
- **Rook:** infection in the reopened back; last paper D+51; "holding" D+52–55; Sowerby opens
  the foot of the stitches D+56 and lets the yellow out; "beginning to come in". On his face in
  the **second bed on the left** at least to D+63. Did no power work in M6.
- **Pell:** right ankle broken; on a frame of its own in the ward; sends "concur" by Toren.
- **Dee:** two fingers over her floor D+50, a finger and a bit after drawing Jab once; "a hand
  over" by D+55 after five nights. **Draws nobody for twenty days from D+57** (Director + Sowerby).
- **Callie:** two left ribs cracked (M5); shaking in the cold D+52; lee stopped at 6 breaths D+54;
  at her floor D+55 with a nosebleed; re-strapped by Keel D+55; "six weeks for the ribs" (Keel,
  per Dessa). Own reserve otherwise recovered.
- **Jab:** the right palm's fixed white patch (a burn; will scar; no feeling there); the blister
  **broke** D+55; salt water twice daily; lodging warmth stopped at the wrist D+55 by his own
  *off*. Floor a finger; own a hand D+55 after the draw.
- **Toren:** left forearm in its cuff (Sowerby not shown examining it in M6); own to a turn at
  the end of D+55; not below his floor.
- **Wyck:** cleared D+56; took a husk on the bar D+55 without striking; strap status after
  clearance not shown. **Yard lifter (second):** hound bite on the calf D+55. **Yard lifter
  (first):** swallowed burden, sick at the 4th hour D+55, drawn first. **Katori:** in the fog
  since noon D+55, drawn. **Otto, the girl, Ines:** drawn. **The day holder:** in her chair at
  the lip on D+60 (Sowerby said no eight times of nine).

## Objects

- **Cart:** reassembled D+50; the walking wheel (wedged, tallow-greased); rope traces; the
  carrier on its tailboard sledge. At the shed from D+55.
- **Jab's frame:** the jars returned D+58; six empties clinking in the straw.
- **Little:** returned to Tonk's pillow D+55 night.

  sheet with seven signatures.
- **Pell's chalk** (Toren has it); the brass glass (Dee); the second counter (Callie).
- **The brass chit:** with Rook, face down under his hand; Hallet wants it back "for the ledger".
- **The disk:** on Toren's chest under his shirt, cold; not taken out on the page.

## Residency, defender terms and the breach ruling (D+57)

- **Breach:** read from the paper; Toren and Dee each say it in their own words. The Director:
  a rule broken **in secret** "is a rule that's gone"; one **written on the day, told to her
  first, both names on it** is an **owned exception**. Written: *D+48. The signed line at Caul
  Hill crossed, on purpose, by T. Voss and D. Wren together, said aloud and priced beforehand,
  three Handfuls, inside two turns. Owned the same night. Censured, both names. Not praised. Not
  a model. The red-call rule and the abort line stand as signed, unchanged.* Recovery/restriction:
  **Dee draws nobody for 20 days** (offered 20 herself); **Toren leads no party past the Sag
  under his own name before the spring review** ("You'll lead a lane. You'll lead a watch").
- **Household reviews (clocks restarted, terms lengthened):**
  - *C. and T.* — **resident a year**, on Tilda's paid tally work and Callie's defender
    standing; reviewed next autumn, once.
  - *J. and T.* — Tonk's settlement until he's done growing stays a **debt the station owes
    (written D+38) and is not on this sheet**: "It's not yours to trade and it's not mine to take
    back." Jab's own residency his own, **reviewed once a year**, on no service.
  - **The rest of the yard (40 households) remain on 28-day reviews.** "This is what one hill
    buys. Two households. Not forty."
- **Jab's terms (his own, signed *J.*):** *First: supper at the fifth bed. Daily. (T.'s term.)*;
  **no** to the medical roll/on call ("It's a hill every day. There's nobody to say *off*");
  **yes** to once-weekly teaching on the other side of Dee's table (reaffirmed; first hour D+57,
  nobody came); **offered**: two Sag watches a week this winter as a Sustain worker with a named
  watch (Dee or Rook), never alone, floor recorded in Dessa's book each time — "His own offer. Not
  on call. Not on the roll. Not sent for."
- **Defender standing, provisional, reviewed in the spring:** not a contract; each signs their own
  line; **an adult on every watch** (Mr Marsh, Miss Wren or Katori); floors and injuries in the
  book; anyone may come off in spring, no debt, no clock; a watch stood alone voids the sheet.
  Signed: **T. Voss, C., D. Cole, J., Otto, W., the weighbridge girl (full name, not shown).**
  Not on it: the yard lifters, Ines, Katori ("You're the watch").
- **The Sag:** 58 arrivals on the Director's slate for the week (26 on D+31).
- **The winter rota (Dessa, D+59):** Toren the first lane mornings; **the three on the second
  watch together** (Jab at the table behind the lamps, Callie on the lip reading, Toren on the
  lane), with Dee after her twenty days and Rook "when he's off his face". Toren: "Because that's
  the line… It's what we are."
- **The Home Line** (see *Author choices*): the company's name, under Dessa's command.

## The disk clue (exact) and the Cinder trace (exact)

- **Disk clue (ch 46, Callie's POV; ch 48, Toren's):** in the machine's rack, at the end of the
  first row beside the empty place of the dead reference box (row one, nine along), a **shallow
  round seat in the steel, two thumbs wide, rimmed, with nine fine raised ribs from centre to
  rim — empty, and the only bright, film-free steel on the rack**. The technician: "Not in my
  time. Not in my father's. It came empty." The machine's **fittings book** (wheel mark on the
  cover), first page: one printed line with a number, an unknown word and the figure *9*, struck
  through in pencil; in the margin in the same pencil, in "a small upright hand" not his: ***released
  to bearer***. "It was taken out before the machine ever came here… *Bearer.* Whoever had it in
  his hand." Toren's right hand goes flat on his shirt over his breastbone, unknowing; "When?" —
  "It doesn't say." Callie sees and keeps it. Ch 48: Toren, alone, hand deliberately on the cold
  disk; "*Released.*… *Bearer.* Whoever had it in his hand. He had it." He does not take it out.
  **Not stated:** that the disk fits the socket, what the socket did, who wrote the note, the
  Makers, the Satori link, origin or meaning.
- **Cinder trace (ch 48):** an arrival party of four from the east road came in at the south gate
  on D+56; one tried to pay intake at Hallet's window with **a brass chit** ("good anywhere on the
  east road"), paid in station coin instead, and left the chit on the ledge. Hallet keeps it
  "because of the stamp" and brings it to Rook D+59. Toren sees letters he does not know — **not
  four** — and a number **not six**. Rook turns it face down: "**It's the same brass.**" The four
  are in the south huts. Rook: "Then we'll see." **No name, shape, leader or intent revealed; no
  confrontation.**

## Withheld truths (still withheld at book's end)

- Dee's exact door count; Rook's third door (Resilience) and unbound state; **Katori's unsaid
  words** ("I've not said the rest" — recalled by Toren D+60, not delivered); Satori's origin; the
  Makers; **Cinder's new name and shape**; the full meaning of the disk and the socket; what the
  sky over Caul Hill is doing or what is in the drum; whether the lifter kept the sky's time or
  the reverse; **whether the Sag surge was caused by Caul Hill** (never asserted; "the same shape.
  That's all anybody can write"); what the ten unvisited rooms hold; the weighbridge girl's name.

## Knowledge boundaries at exit

- **Known and shown:** the sky over Caul Hill still has no count (seen from the wash D+50); loose
  ash east of the hill mostly lies still, sliding occasionally at no count; the north road's
  husk lines increased and ditch-grey lies combed south; the Sag surged D+55 in pushes at no count
  and then lay down; the Sag on D+60 is ordinary white fog, no count; the machine is honest.
- **Not known:** cause of any of it; whether the surge will come again; the state of the Caul Hill
  drifts beyond the wash.

## Deliberately absent or withheld in this movement

- **No new continuing named character.** New unnamed: the east-road arrival party of four;
  three south-hut young people in the first lane (names in Dessa's book, not on the page).
  Retired: an invented crew name was removed during the check.
- **No home Flask opened; no infinite Yield; no soul theft; no time reversal; no universal
  cure; no surprise extra door; no romance; no death.** Sustain drew only active burden (fixed:
  Jab's palm, the tech's hand, Senna's nerves, Tilda's growth, Tonk's plate). Ward moved only
  matter. Guard paid by breath and load; Edge only after release.
- **No Guard+Edge blend.** The hold is shorter and simpler than Caul Hill: no named creature,
  no Yield.

## Final hooks for Book Three

1. **Caul Hill:** the ash "where it lies", the half-open 140 box, three vent jars, ten unentered
   rooms, the drum, a sky with no count, the green-wire gate "so they would come", the rope at
   the wash "for somebody coming the other way". Somebody will go back — "Not before the spring.
   Not under his name."
2. **The Sag:** surges at no count; 58 arrivals a week; two holders (one on his face, one in a
   chair); the Home Line on a winter rota; Jab's watches at the table behind the lamps.
3. **The brass:** four men from the east road in the south huts; Rook's "Then we'll see."
4. **The disk:** an empty nine-ribbed socket, *released to bearer*, and a boy who is the bearer.
5. **Katori's rest**, still held.
6. **The yard:** three lanes, a seven-column ledger with reasons, Senna's chair, Jab's table, a
   spring review of standing, forty households still on twenty-eight days.
7. **Bodies:** Tonk's frame till spring and his future operation; Tilda's growth and the folder
   under her hand; Senna's course every eleven days; Rook's back; Pell's ankle; Callie's ribs
   (six weeks); Jab's scar; the technician's left hand.

## Length and surface facts (for review, not scores)

- **Scene breaks:** 90 `---` markers across 8 chapters, about 470 words per section (short
  against the formula's ~950 proxy; same direction as every earlier movement).
- **"The way …"** occurs about 123 times (including literal uses), against 141 in the repaired
  Movement Five. "Nobody said anything" occurs 9 times.
- **Formula comparison** (sentence distribution, Flesch, development-beat cadence, progression-
  vocabulary placement) was **not run**; it belongs to the editorial pass.

## PROJECT EVIDENCE — provenance/MOVEMENT-003-CONTINUITY.md

# Movement Three — continuity checkpoint

Author run: Claude Opus 5.5 (`claude-opus-5-5`), Monroe Jackson 1.3.0 / O'Connor 1.3.0.
This is a working note for movement review and Movement Four planning. It is not
manuscript text, and it is not an editorial score. It supersedes
`MOVEMENT-003-WORKING-PLAN.md`, the pre-drafting plan, wherever the two differ.

## Author choices that are now canon

| Question | Resolution on the page | Where |
|---|---|---|
| Handfuls in a Flask | A clean Flask is eight Handfuls at the weighbridge; a thin one is nearer seven. It is written inside the store book's cover | 19 |
| Where the mission's ash comes from | The station store under the weighbridge, lent rather than given. It held 31 Flasks and 1 Handful on D+31 and 31 Flasks and 2½ Handfuls (4 thin, 27 clean) on D+34 after the D+33 lift. It did not move again before departure. Twelve clean Flasks were weighed out by Tilda on D+39; she sent two thin jars back. The board reads *LENT. CAUL HILL. 12 FLASKS, CLEAN. J., 8, IN THE FRAME. P., 4, ON THE CART. ROAD 1. HOME 3.* | 18, 19, 21, 23 |
| The east-shed allotment (repair) | The yard's medical/training ash is a separate allotment, signed out of the weighbridge store to the east shed before the D+25 lift and therefore not part of the D+31 count. The long-table quarter-Handfuls, the tenths, the settled Handfuls, the pen's six clean Flasks (19 Handfuls spent) and the D+36 knee/arm draws all come out of it. Unspent pen ash went back to the shed rack, not the store. Stated on the page in ch 17 (the rack "signed out of the weighbridge store before the lift"), ch 20 (the bench rack) and ch 21 (Dee: "Don't count it twice") | 17, 20, 21 |
| The watch's dirty jars (repair) | Otto's jars are sweepings from spawn that came apart on the made lip itself, above the lamp-line, where they can be brushed up without kneeling below an unheld line. They are dirty yard samples and training stock: weighed into the weighman's own book, shelved in the east shed marked *dirty*, and never counted as store income or as a resumed lift. Everything that comes apart below the lamp-line still "lies where it falls" | 19, 23 |
| The red-call rule (repair) | Written by Dee the night of the pen, copied under the lines D+37, and read to the Director D+38. *Red, and nobody trapped: everybody out, at once. Red, and a body trapped: everybody not needed to get that body out or cover the source goes out at once; the fewest who can do it stay, named aloud by the watch as they go in, only until the person is out and the source covered, then out too; nobody back in until the counter is under the red. Every time, that night, in the book: who stayed, how long each was in, what the counter said at each, and why.* Dee owns that at the pen nobody obeyed *red*; by the rule Rook and (after the cloths) Sowerby should have left. The Director signs it and says nobody is praised on her paper for staying | 21 |
| Rook's prior recall | On Senna, by feel, for three hundred miles on the road, fuelled by a flask of road-ash. He vented into the ground through his heels without knowing he was venting. It made his hands ache for an hour afterward. It is why Sowerby's "it shouldn't have worked" held | 19, 20 |
| Why Rook lacks the capacity method | At Homura, Vell's long table belonged to the medical line (nine children the year Rook came). Rook was in the doors line with Vera and Ander. At fifteen he called the table tedious behind Vell's chair; Vell hit him across the legs with his stick and said he'd have to learn it off a child | 17 |
| How Dee learned what to find | Every spring Vell held his own forearm to a small lead-housed source for a count of sixty and had the new medical children find and draw it. Dee sat at the table "for the months I had there" (this fits "since I was fourteen") | 19 |
| The exposure apparatus | The dead reference box from row one, nine along (the one Toren carried on D+11). Its reader was dead and its sealed source was live, weakened by seventy years. The planted failure: the technician had taken seven of its eleven back-plate screws over twenty years "for the rack" | 19, 20 |
| What active burden feels like to Jab | "The busy thing that isn't them": warm, not the person's own warmth, like a stone left in the sun or a fine warm grit. Fixed damage feels like Dessa's "old leaning wall", which is not busy and cannot be drawn | 20 |
| Recall mechanics as staged | Draw like lifting a drift: flat palm, no hauling. Ash paid in through the left hand passes out through the right and loosens the burden, which comes up into the right palm as prickling heat. Hold it high so it does not go down into you, then vent it into spent, colourless ash in a vent jar. Clean in the left, dirty out the right, never the same shelf. The watch draws the leftovers out of the worker before the worker rests | 20 |
| Signs of lodging | The healer's palms go red "like he'd held a pan", and the counter reads high at the palm after venting. Rook lodged at about the 20th minute and came off at the 27th | 20 |
| Jab's door | It passes ash even with an empty reserve (D+29). "Carrying" means holding ash high in the palm, and he managed 11 breaths on D+29, 20 on D+33 and 40 by D+39. His pass-through jar method, left hand on the jar and right hand on the patient, never touches his own reserve until the jar runs dry, and then it takes him instantly | 17, 19, 20 |
| Callie's lock | She settles very well, then cannot spend what she has settled. It released during the combined drill (D+34), when she let the Ward go while Toren held the load. From then on she can fund the hook's Ward from settled ash: 1½ turns on a quarter-Handful (D+34), 1¾ turns (D+39), and an estimated ~7 minutes on a settled Handful. On herself the Ward costs about three times as much, and she cannot have it on herself and in the hook at once | 19, 23 |
| Toren's intake | The slowest in the yard: a tenth of a Handful in 104 breaths, and a Handful in "most of an hour" the night before departure. He must always be "the first one full, the night before". A tenth of a Handful settled funds about four soft holds at six paces | 18, 24 |
| Settling times, others | Callie takes about 30 breaths for a tenth, ~70–75 for a quarter and ~300 for a Handful. The weighbridge girl takes about 40 for a tenth and ~100 for a quarter | 18, 19, 23 |
| Ward and radiation | **Not stated on the page.** On the hill Callie's Ward holds doors and stairs against creatures only. Recommend the owner keep it that way: Ward does not shield radiation | — |
| The dummy | Otto named the straw training dummy "Norris". It is a sack, not a person. Flagged in case the owner counts it as a name | 19 |

You know where I am") and gives Jab Little ("You're only borrowing him"; "don't eat inside"). Toren: "Everybody back." The gate: the day holder in a chair; Katori and Rook ("Come back, Elias. I've not said the rest"); Toren tells Dessa "pull the door" and she writes it on the departure page, with six lines left for *back*. The home line leaves first: Otto pulls Dessa to the south lip in the barrow (Wyck beside it) and sets her on the watch's crate; the party waits at the gate until Katori raises her blade. The party crosses the unheld Sag on Dessa's "Go now." At the north lip: "Persons in party. Six. And everybody in the beds." | 24 |

## The north-gate pen (D+35), as staged

- **Positions:**
  - The pen is 40 × 20 ft with the drain in the middle (ch 1). The box sits on an iron depot stool 18 in from the edge of the drain, window toward the gate end, inside a chalk ring 3 ft out from the stool; on the drain side the chalk crosses the near edge of the grating.
  - The tech inside the ring, with elbow gloves and tongs.
  - Dee kneeling just outside the chalk on the window side, her bare right forearm held into the ring a foot from the box; Jab beside her; Rook on a crate behind Jab.
  - Six clean Flasks on the bench, from the east-shed allotment, not the store; three vent jars of spent ash on the floor by the gate wall.
  - Callie on the bench at the hatch end, about 20 ft off, with the counter and Dee's glass.
  - Geometry checks: Jab reaches the kneeling tech's arm from the ring's edge; Callie's four-foot lever over the stool rim keeps her hands outside the ring; the capsule rolls 18 in from the stool to the grating; the hatch end is about 20 ft from the stool. Toren is the only helper who crosses the chalk before the recall team closes on the tech.
  - Sowerby at the hatch end with a basin, cloths, scissors and blankets. Hallet behind his window.
  - Toren at the pen gate as runner; two grey coats outside with the hose.
- **Rules said aloud and written:** only the tech's hands on the box; only Dee's arm at the window; one turn of the glass; ticks every ten breaths; *red* means everybody out; *shut* means the arm comes away; Jab touches only Dee and Rook touches only Jab; clean in the left, dirty out the right; *vent*, *off* and *floor*.
- **The planned exposure:** 40–44 ticks per ten breaths at the bench over one turn. Jab felt the burden in Dee's arm without touching it.
- **The failure:**
  1. The slide stuck a third closed. The tech shoved, the box turned, and the four-screw back plate sheared.
  2. The lead plug and the capsule fell out. The box tipped, and the tech caught it on his right hand.
  3. The capsule split on the drain grating and grey-white powder spilled, some of it into the air.
  4. The counter went to a buzz; Callie called *red*.
- **Containment:**
  1. Dee: "Nobody breathe it", and Sowerby threw wet cloths.
  2. Toren Strided in and put up a ground strut against the rocking box: "Holding".
  3. Callie came down the bench side, reading the floor ("It's a bowl… Bench side's clean"). She used the hook as a lever over the stool rim to lift the box's front edge 1½ in, then put the Ward in the hook: "Taking" / "Yours" / "Mine".
  4. Jab pulled the tech's hand out; the glove stayed behind. He dragged the tech by the collar along the bench side to the hatch end.
  5. Toren went back under the box: "I'm under it". Callie released the Ward into a short Edge on a count of two. From five feet she cut the powdered sleeve open cuff to shoulder without touching skin, and Sowerby stripped the coat.
  6. Toren walked the lead case on its edge over the capsule. The counter fell from a buzz to 90 per ten breaths.
- **Recall:**
  - Sowerby washed the tech first. Dee: "Rook works, Jab under him, I'm the watch and the reserve, Callie the glass and ticks, Toren the jars." Start 9:23 in the evening by Hallet.
  - Chest ticks: 140 → 120 → 100 → 85 → 70.
  - Rook drew at the belly and vented into the floor until Dee stopped him ("Not into my floor"); the ticks under his knees read 50 against a background of 8. He lodged and came off at 27 minutes.
  - Jab worked alone with Dee as reserve and watch. The jar ran dry mid-draw and the pass-through took from Jab's own reserve. **He called "Floor" and took his own hand off.** The exact minute was not recorded; Dee's sheet says "a few minutes after Mr Marsh came off" (between 27 and 40).
  - The tech vomited at 40 min and shook at 44. At 48 Jab could not draw the hand: it was fixed. End at 52 min; chest 12, room 7–8.
  - Dee then drew the leftovers out of Jab and, later, about an hour's worth out of Rook.
- **The red call:** nobody left. The breach is reckoned on D+38 (see the red-call rule above).
- **Cost:** 2 Flasks and 3 Handfuls from the east-shed jars (19 Handfuls), Dee's settled Handful, and Rook to his floor. The weighbridge store is not debited. Callie's hook was taken for washing ("Third time, miss") and came back tagged *pen, 35th*.
- **Aftermath and message:**
  - Everyone was hosed at the gate like arrivals, and Hallet came out of his door.
  - The tech's message: "Tell the boy the ink'll come off now."
  - The tech's toll: right-hand damage (fixed), sickness for about a week, and slower work at the desk.

## The ledger and the Director's permission (D+37–38)

- **Ash:**
  - 12 clean Flasks lent: 1 for the road there; 3 for the road home, cached with Pell at the cart outside the fence and never to go inside; 8 for inside the fence.
  - That makes 9 committed and 3 untouchable. Nothing is counted from the hill.
- **Rates (guesses, to be corrected by the counter):**
  - Based on Senna's dictated record as she gave it: eleven days inside, five or six hours a day; her fingertips began to go on the sixth day; the deepest workers lost most. Nobody on the page now says the fence "took her hands in eleven days" as fact. Senna's own "it took my hands and my feet and twenty years" stays as her framing.
  - About 1 Handful per person per hour near the gate and 3 deep.
  - Five people go inside; Pell stays out.
  - Inside budget: approach 5, deep rooms 30 (two hours), out 5, venting losses 8, whatever they meet 8 = 56 Handfuls, about 7 Flasks, called 8.
- **Abort-line arithmetic (Callie, D+37):** 8 Flasks inside = 64 Handfuls. The turn line is 2 Flasks left = 16. That leaves 48 to work with, exactly the priced working budget (people 40 + venting 8). The 8 for "whatever we meet" is contingency, the first of the last two Flasks; if it is being spent, the party is already coming out. The last Flask protects extraction. Anything met early comes off the 48: the work gets shorter and the line does not move.
- **Why one mission (Callie's words, ledgered):** the boxes lie down the yellow stair in the main building, under the wrong sky. Eight Flasks buys one room, maybe two. Anything more has to be paid for with ash lifted under the rip. "It's only two things on paper."
- **Rotation:** work, reserve and watch in 20-minute turns (the glass turned 20 times). The order is Dee, then Jab, then Rook, and Rook works 10 minutes (half a turn). The watch draws the leftovers out of the worker. Vent jars are sealed and left on the hill.
- **Lines:** everybody out when 2 Flasks are left inside; any healer at their floor twice; any healer still warm after the watch has drawn them; two healers off; Jab below his floor once and Dee turns the party round.
- **If Rook can't do his share:** Dee and Jab continue with Callie as watch. The deep window halves to one hour, and if the boxes are not in the first room within that hour, the party comes out without them.
- **Roles:**
  - Callie: reads the floor for dust and water and picks the clean way; holds a door or stair with the hook's Ward off settled ash; is the watch if Rook is off; carries the second counter.
  - Toren: runner; carries jars from Jab's frame; ground strut under anything that falls; heel light to fence hounds; knows where everybody is and shouts.
  - Boxes travel only in the lead carrier on the cart, never on a back.
- **Travelling:** Rook, Dee, Pell (navigator, cart, cache, record of who goes in and out), Jab, Callie and Toren, with Duchess and the crew wagon.
- **Home line:**
  - Dessa is in command.
  - The watch: Katori (blade), Ines (bow), Otto, the weighbridge girl, and the two yard lifters (one bitten D+30), whom Wyck is teaching to stand.
  - Wyck asked to go; Keel says D+56.
  - Watch record: 38 arrivals in 11 days (31 husks, 7 hounds) and one bite. With no lift, about a Flask a day goes into the ditch; that is the station's price.
- **The Director's conditions:**
  - The ash is lent, not given, every jar against a name and chalked back on the board.
  - The watch must hold; if it fails before departure, nobody goes.
  - Every travelling household's review is held over, its clock stopped at the gate until return.
  - Dee's word is the Director's on the road.
  - The red-call rule is signed; nobody is praised on her paper for staying in the pen.
  - The permission is narrow: the live boxes with the wheel (two, three if possible), in lead; count the sky and bring back the count; lift at the edge of the rip if the ledger requires; **do not go into it or looking for what is under it**. If it comes to them, do what they must and come home.
- **Signatures:** E. M., D. W., P. (with "concur", as in his D+37 margin note, but this time as one of the travellers), C., J., K., D. C., W. (left-handed) and T. Voss, under Senna's signature and the Director's cross. Pell's line in the D+22 page, under "Everybody out", now reads "Everybody back."

## Jab's terms (D+38)

1. Tonk is settled until he is done growing, whatever happens to Jab. The Director wrote it as a debt the station owes (for the technician and the holder), not as a bargain with Jab.
2. Learning at Dee's table is not signing.
3. The register records what he can do. His line reads *His own.*
4. Nobody sends for him, not in visiting hours, not on picture days, not after bad nights. People may ask; he may say no, and the no goes in the margin. He chooses whom he goes to.
5. He goes to the hill because of the shed, not because he is sent. The Director: "I haven't sent anybody. I've let."

**The Director's own condition:** after he returns, once a week he sits "on the other side" of Dee's table and teaches whoever comes. This is not on call and not on the roll. He said yes. She will ask about the roll again when he is back.

## Object state at exit

- **Jab's frame:** rebuilt from the two halves (in twelve since D+2). The split lattice is replaced with new ash cross-pieces, lashed tight and short. A rack of 8 canvas-lined sockets sits where Tonk's seat was. The curved ash rail is kept, and the top row of jars leans on it. The belt sits high on the hip bone and the chest band under the yoke. It carries the 8 inside jars.
- **The long cord:** untied from the sixth-bed rail by Tonk on D+41 and coiled on Tonk's own fist; Tonk keeps both ends. Jab has no cord.
- **Little (the fish):** with Jab, inside his coat, left side, buttoned. "Borrowed", to be brought back.
- **Other gear:**
  - The second counter (older than the tech) is with Callie in a canvas bag from Dessa.
  - The first counter stays at the shed.
  - Dee's brass glass and book, and Pell's atlas and route sheet, travel with the party.
  - The lead carrier (empty, made by the shed's two unnamed assistants from depot sheet) is on the cart.
- **Dessa's ledger:**
  - The D+32 route page, signed with the cross in a circle.
  - The D+37 ledger and why-one-mission entry, Callie's abort-line sum, the note that pen ash came from the shed allotment, and Dee's red-call rule copied under the lines.
  - *Home line, command: D. Cole* in Toren's hand.
  - The D+41 departure page ("12 Flasks lent, clean, weighed by Tilda…"), with *S. V.: pull the door*, which Toren dictated to her at the gate, and six blank lines for *back*.
  - A private line she wrote at the bottom, unseen.
- **The copied plate and the signed paper:** in Toren's coat, with his stone in the other pocket.
- **The weighbridge board:** the LENT line; "SUSSTAIN" boxed with a ruler.
- **The dead box, capsule and pen floor square:** in the depot.

## Open clocks at exit (D+41)

- **Machine:** the tech guesses by D+54 and says guessing isn't stopping; Sowerby can dose Senna from her blood for a few cycles, badly. D+43's cycle is on, with the tech working left-handed, two orderlies lifting Senna into the chair and one of them pushing it (Senna complaining about the camber). The next cycle is about D+54.
- **Reviews:** Callie/Tilda (D+41) and Jab (D+42) are **held over**; the clocks stopped at the gate.
- **The watch:** holding without a holder, and no lifts. Arrivals were 26 and then 27 a week.
- **Bodies:**
  - The tech's hand: Sowerby knows in three weeks (about D+57).
  - Wyck is cleared D+56.
- **The party:** left on the morning of D+41, after the eighth-hour ward visit and after the home line had taken its Sag posts. It is 6 days to the fence (north on the old north road for two days, then west on the old line), and it expects to be back no earlier than about D+53.

## Bodies at exit

- **Tonk:**
  - Infection clear; the frame stays until spring; the growth plate is still worse.
  - The chair transfer, most of the way, took 11 min (D+39) and 9 min (D+41); the nurse takes the last hand's width.
  - Angry with Jab D+39–40; released him by his own choice D+41.
- **Tilda:**
  - After D+40: a 131-s fit, then loss of the right side, words and Callie's name. The name came back that evening.
  - D+41: she walked to the ward on her stick with the right leg lagging; the right hand closed partly and shakily on a cup.
  - The second medicine was raised. She keeps the number: she put her hand on the shut folder D+40, and Callie did not ask.
- **Senna:**
  - She can no longer take the transfer step and is lifted by two (D+32). Her hands and feet are numb.
  - She is the author of the route and exposure ledger; the Director came to her bed.
  - "A road I can plan and never walk" (D+41).
- **Rook:**
  - Rose without hands D+31 and in one movement D+41. Held ten minutes D+33.
  - Paired recall D+35: lodged, palms burned red, pink by D+41.
  - Margin reduced (Dee has measured it); share set at half turns.
  - The back wound is still healing at departure; Toren walks a pace behind him on his left.
- **Dee:** planned arm exposure drawn D+36. No other injury.
- **Jab:**
  - Bucket gone D+28–30; back from D+31 (hook light unasked).
  - D+35 near his floor after the pen, by his own call. "A hand and a bit" D+39; floor still a finger.
  - Hands steady from D+31. A faint redness in the right palm creases after the pen, drawn by Dee.
- **Toren:**
  - Left forearm splint off D+39; now a laced leather cuff ("knit, not strong").
  - Right palm seam healed.
  - Knee dose drawn D+36.
  - Guard: repeatable soft holds under a controlled load, and one real-load hold (the box). Heel light held through impact.
- **Callie:**
  - Ribs healing (the run on D+38 hurt).
  - Can fund the hook's Ward from settled ash; handoff gap 2 with someone under the load, 3 without. Lock released.
- **Wyck:** strapped until D+56; teaching the watch to stand; will take Toren's sack at eight paces.
- **Dessa:** leg unchanged; home command.
- **The day holder:** in the east corridor; wears her coat; out in a chair at the gate for 20 min D+41; advised on the Sag's break points.
- **The weighbridge girl:** left wrist in plaster; uses her blade right-handed on the watch.
- **Yard lifter:** bitten on the arm D+30, stitched.

## Deliberately absent or withheld

- **Not revealed:** Dee's door count (she shows only Sustain and her reserve work) and Rook's third door. Katori's "why the same word" is untold and explicitly deferred ("I've not said the rest"). No disk, no Cinder, no Sustain-echo creature.
- **Not shown:** the rip, which is unseen; no group Ward; no full moving Guard.
- **No new continuing named characters.** Unnamed: the Director, the tech, his two assistants, the long coat, the day holder, the weighbridge girl, the weighman, the yard lifters. See the note on Norris above.
- **No romance.**
- **Milo's grief:** Senna's nightly naming (Tonk silent on D+39 because he is angry) and the ledger's first page. Not declared resolved.
- **Owner-level conflicts not touched:** Wyck's side is left as page state (left hand for the bar and his initial). Toren's original break is not restated. Tonk's weeks are not counted: one draft line was removed so as not to touch the six-versus-ten question.

| **Total** | | **64,254** | **64,995** |

The repair is recorded in `editor/MOVEMENT-003-REPAIR-REPORT.md`. The prose below describes the first draft; viewpoint shares moved by less than half a point.

- **Length:** 64,254 words against the packet's approximate 46,000–52,000, about 24% over the upper bound. No scene was padded, and none was compressed to fit. The lowest-cost places to cut, if the owner wants them:
  - the opening of ch 18, which recaps the paper;
  - the ledger-building in ch 21, which the Director scene then restates;
  - some of the lane work in ch 19.
- **Viewpoint share, Movement Three:** Jab 36.6%, Toren 36.2%, Callie 27.3%.
  - By chapter count the movement leans Jab (3 chapters, including both Sustain pivots). By words, Jab and Toren are level and Callie is behind.
  - This deviates from "Callie and Toren co-equal behind him". Callie's two chapters are the longest, but she has one fewer chapter.
- **Cumulative through Movement Three** (167,076 words): Callie about 34.1%, Toren about 34.2%, Jab about 31.8%.
- **Scene breaks:** 125 `---` markers, about 514 words per section. That is fewer per 10k than Movement Two, but still well above the formula's ~950-word pause proxy; flagged for the editorial pass.
- **Tics watched:** zero uses of "the woman with the good boots", "face did nothing at all, which was how", "tipped plate" and "about four seconds".
